	  <!-- Hindi / English Translation -->
<script type="text/javascript">

    function googleTranslateElementInit() {
       new google.translate.TranslateElement({
        pageLanguage: 'en',
        includedLanguages: 'en,hi,te',
        autoDisplay: false
    }, 'google_translate_element');
    }

    function translateLanguage(lang) {
        var select = document.querySelector('.goog-te-combo');

        if (!select) {
            console.log("Google Translate is not loaded yet.");
            return;
        }

        select.value = lang;

        select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
    }

</script>

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

    var dropdownButtons = document.querySelectorAll(
        ".a11y-dropdown > .a11y-btn"
    );

    dropdownButtons.forEach(function (button) {

        button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {

            event.stopPropagation();

            var dropdown = button.closest(".a11y-dropdown");

            // Close other dropdowns
            document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown.open")
                .forEach(function (item) {
                    if (item !== dropdown) {
                        item.classList.remove("open");
                    }
                });

            // Toggle current dropdown
            dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
        });

    });


    /* Prevent clicking inside dropdown from closing it */
    document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown-menu")
        .forEach(function (menu) {

            menu.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                event.stopPropagation();
            });

        });


    /* Click anywhere outside = close */
    document.addEventListener("click", function () {

        document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown.open")
            .forEach(function (dropdown) {
                dropdown.classList.remove("open");
            });

    });

});


function closeA11yDropdown(button) {
    var dropdown = button.closest(".a11y-dropdown");

    if (dropdown) {
        dropdown.classList.remove("open");
    }
}
document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown-menu button")
    .forEach(function (button) {

        button.addEventListener("click", function () {

            var dropdown = button.closest(".a11y-dropdown");

            if (dropdown) {
                dropdown.classList.remove("open");
            }

        });

    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
function getXHR(){
        var xmlHttp=null;
		if (window.XMLHttpRequest) {
            xmlHttp= new XMLHttpRequest();
        } else if (window.ActiveXObject) {
			xmlHttp = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
        }
        return xmlHttp;
	}
	function formatter() {
	
		var xmlhttp= getXHR();
		
		var  disObj= document.getElementById('distance');
		var depthObj = document.getElementById('depth');
		var latlongObj = document.getElementById('latlong');
		var dis = disObj.options[disObj.selectedIndex].value;
		
		var depth = depthObj.options[depthObj.selectedIndex].value;
		var latlong = latlongObj.options[latlongObj.selectedIndex].value;
		
		var url= "formattedForecast.action?distanceformat="+dis+"&depthformat="+depth+"&latlongformat="+latlong;
		
			   	xmlhttp.onreadystatechange=function() {
                   if (xmlhttp.readyState==4) {
					      if (xmlhttp.status==200) {
					   		 var responseData = xmlhttp.responseText;
							 try{
							 	document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML = '';
								document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML =responseData;
								//alert(responseData);
								}catch(e){
								 alert(e.message);
							 }
						  }
						 }
				};
		document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML = '<img src="images/loading-1.gif"  height="242" width="150" />';
		xmlhttp.open("GET", url, true);
	    xmlhttp.send(null); 
	}
	function download(doc){
	var format = doc;
	var distObj = document.getElementById('distance');
	var depthObj = document.getElementById('depth');
	var latlongObj = document.getElementById('latlong');
	var dist = distObj.options[distObj.selectedIndex].value;
	var depth = depthObj.options[depthObj.selectedIndex].value;
	var latlong = latlongObj.options[latlongObj.selectedIndex].value;
	
	//alert(dist+"\t"+depth+"\t"+latlong);
	window.open("download.action?documentType="+format+"&distanceformat="+dist+"&depthformat="+depth+"&latlongformat="+latlong,"_self",false);

}
function printDoc(){
	/*

	var satmsg = document.getElementById("satmsg").innerHTML;
	var data = document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML;

	var toPrint = "<p>"+secname+"</p><p>"+satmsg+"</p>"+data+address;
         var popupWin = window.open('', '_blank');
           popupWin.document.open();
           popupWin.document.write("<html><head><link rel='stylesheet' type='text/css' href='./css/PfzStyleSheet.css' /></head><body onLoad='self.print();self.close();'>" + toPrint + "</body></html>");
           popupWin.document.close();
	*/
	var advisory = document.getElementById("mfsadvisory").innerHTML;
	var secname = document.getElementById("sectorname").innerHTML;
	var satmsg = document.getElementById("satmsg").innerHTML;
	var data = document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML;
	var address = document.getElementById("address").innerHTML;
	var updatedDate = document.getElementById("updatedDate").innerHTML;
	var toPrint = "<p style='text-align: right;'>Data updated on:"+updatedDate+"</p>"+"<p>"+advisory+"</p>"+"<p>"+secname+"</p><p>"+satmsg+"</p>"+data+address;
	
         var popupWin = window.open('', '_blank');
           popupWin.document.open();
           popupWin.document.write("<html><head><link rel='stylesheet' type='text/css' href='./css/PfzStyleSheet.css' /></head><body onLoad='window.print()'>" + toPrint + "</body></html>");
           popupWin.document.close();

}
	
 </script>

<script type="text/javascript">

    function googleTranslateElementInit() {
       new google.translate.TranslateElement({
        pageLanguage: 'en',
        includedLanguages: 'en,hi,te',
        autoDisplay: false
    }, 'google_translate_element');
    }

    function translateLanguage(lang) {
        var select = document.querySelector('.goog-te-combo');

        if (!select) {
            console.log("Google Translate is not loaded yet.");
            return;
        }

        select.value = lang;

        select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
    }

</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

    var dropdownButtons = document.querySelectorAll(
        ".a11y-dropdown > .a11y-btn"
    );

    dropdownButtons.forEach(function (button) {

        button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {

            event.stopPropagation();

            var dropdown = button.closest(".a11y-dropdown");

            // Close other dropdowns
            document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown.open")
                .forEach(function (item) {
                    if (item !== dropdown) {
                        item.classList.remove("open");
                    }
                });

            // Toggle current dropdown
            dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
        });

    });


    /* Prevent clicking inside dropdown from closing it */
    document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown-menu")
        .forEach(function (menu) {

            menu.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                event.stopPropagation();
            });

        });


    /* Click anywhere outside = close */
    document.addEventListener("click", function () {

        document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown.open")
            .forEach(function (dropdown) {
                dropdown.classList.remove("open");
            });

    });

});


function closeA11yDropdown(button) {
    var dropdown = button.closest(".a11y-dropdown");

    if (dropdown) {
        dropdown.classList.remove("open");
    }
}
document.querySelectorAll(".a11y-dropdown-menu button")
    .forEach(function (button) {

        button.addEventListener("click", function () {

            var dropdown = button.closest(".a11y-dropdown");

            if (dropdown) {
                dropdown.classList.remove("open");
            }

        });

    });
</script>
  
   




<head>
<script type="text/javascript">
function getXHR(){
        var xmlHttp=null;
		if (window.XMLHttpRequest) {
            xmlHttp= new XMLHttpRequest();
        } else if (window.ActiveXObject) {
			xmlHttp = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
        }
        return xmlHttp;
	}
	function formatter() {
	
		var xmlhttp= getXHR();
		
		var  disObj= document.getElementById('distance');
		var depthObj = document.getElementById('depth');
		var latlongObj = document.getElementById('latlong');
		var dis = disObj.options[disObj.selectedIndex].value;
		
		var depth = depthObj.options[depthObj.selectedIndex].value;
		var latlong = latlongObj.options[latlongObj.selectedIndex].value;
		
		var url= "formattedForecast.action?distanceformat="+dis+"&depthformat="+depth+"&latlongformat="+latlong;
		
			   	xmlhttp.onreadystatechange=function() {
                   if (xmlhttp.readyState==4) {
					      if (xmlhttp.status==200) {
					   		 var responseData = xmlhttp.responseText;
							 try{
							 	document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML = '';
								document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML =responseData;
								//alert(responseData);
								}catch(e){
								 alert(e.message);
							 }
						  }
						 }
				};
		document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML = '<img src="images/loading-1.gif"  height="242" width="150" />';
		xmlhttp.open("GET", url, true);
	    xmlhttp.send(null); 
	}
	function download(doc){
	var format = doc;
	var distObj = document.getElementById('distance');
	var depthObj = document.getElementById('depth');
	var latlongObj = document.getElementById('latlong');
	var dist = distObj.options[distObj.selectedIndex].value;
	var depth = depthObj.options[depthObj.selectedIndex].value;
	var latlong = latlongObj.options[latlongObj.selectedIndex].value;
	
	//alert(dist+"\t"+depth+"\t"+latlong);
	window.open("download.action?documentType="+format+"&distanceformat="+dist+"&depthformat="+depth+"&latlongformat="+latlong,"_self",false);

}
function printDoc(){
	/*
	var secname = document.getElementById("sectorname").innerHTML;
	var satmsg = document.getElementById("satmsg").innerHTML;
	var data = document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML;
	var address = document.getElementById("address").innerHTML;
	
	var toPrint = "<p>"+secname+"</p><p>"+satmsg+"</p>"+data+address;
         var popupWin = window.open('', '_blank');
           popupWin.document.open();
           popupWin.document.write("<html><head><link rel='stylesheet' type='text/css' href='./css/PfzStyleSheet.css' /></head><body onLoad='self.print();self.close();'>" + toPrint + "</body></html>");
           popupWin.document.close();
	*/
	var advisory = document.getElementById("mfsadvisory").innerHTML;
	var secname = document.getElementById("sectorname").innerHTML;
	var satmsg = document.getElementById("satmsg").innerHTML;
	var data = document.getElementById("forecastdata").innerHTML;
	var address = document.getElementById("address").innerHTML;
	var updatedDate = document.getElementById("updatedDate").innerHTML;
	var toPrint = "<p style='text-align: right;'>Data updated on:"+updatedDate+"</p>"+"<p>"+advisory+"</p>"+"<p>"+secname+"</p><p>"+satmsg+"</p>"+data+address;
	
         var popupWin = window.open('', '_blank');
           popupWin.document.open();
           popupWin.document.write("<html><head><link rel='stylesheet' type='text/css' href='./css/PfzStyleSheet.css' /></head><body onLoad='window.print()'>" + toPrint + "</body></html>");
           popupWin.document.close();

}
	
 </script>
